| R021:report:L003 | COMPOSITE_RESEARCH_OR_DISPOSITION | INVENTORIED_COMPOSITE_UNIT_ATOMIC_SUPPORT_UNASSESSED | **Status:** standalone current research report (final). **Prepared:** 2026-10-01. |
| R021:report:L004 | COMPOSITE_RESEARCH_OR_DISPOSITION | INVENTORIED_COMPOSITE_UNIT_ATOMIC_SUPPORT_UNASSESSED | **Assignment:** inputs/TASK.md — investigate the format obligations and implementation requirements that the admitted thin viewer plan (inputs/plan/Viewer.md) needs, over the whole brief (inputs/brief.md), with source scope limited to the frozen normative capture `sources/S003.txt` (OME-Zarr 0.5). No live fetching was performed or permitted. |
| R021:report:L005 | PROPOSAL_OR_UNEXECUTED_TEST | INVENTORIED_COMPOSITE_UNIT_ATOMIC_SUPPORT_UNASSESSED | **Proposed validation: UNEXECUTED** (see §10). No execution evidence exists in this corpus. |
| R021:report:L011 | COMPOSITE_RESEARCH_OR_DISPOSITION | INVENTORIED_COMPOSITE_UNIT_ATOMIC_SUPPORT_UNASSESSED | - All admitted inputs were read in full with the bounded read tool, expanding until every governing source byte was inspected: inputs/brief.md (5 lines), inputs/TASK.md (11 lines), inputs/catalog.json (40 lines), inputs/sources/S003.txt (896 lines, complete), inputs/plan/Viewer.md (21 lines), plus the task card TASK.md (9 lines). |
| R021:report:L012 | COMPOSITE_RESEARCH_OR_DISPOSITION | INVENTORIED_COMPOSITE_UNIT_ATOMIC_SUPPORT_UNASSESSED | - The S003 capture read back with sha256 `5d8b240877ed2cf9596566187bcb3779ea011d5319807134c65e2e7dc2ae82de`, byte-identical to the catalog's `original_sha256`/`view_sha256` (36,980 bytes, 896 lines), so all line locators below are pinned to that verified version. |
| R021:report:L013 | COMPOSITE_RESEARCH_OR_DISPOSITION | INVENTORIED_COMPOSITE_UNIT_ATOMIC_SUPPORT_UNASSESSED | - No external queries, searches, or fetches were executed (the assignment forbids live sources for this bounded case); consequently no claim of absence in the wild is drawn from any search, and none is asserted. |
| R021:report:L014 | COMPOSITE_RESEARCH_OR_DISPOSITION | INVENTORIED_COMPOSITE_UNIT_ATOMIC_SUPPORT_UNASSESSED | - Findings were compiled as F01–F14 (full standalone bodies in `findings.json`); each maps to exact S003 line ranges recorded in `acquisition.json`. False-dismissal checks are in §7. |
| R021:report:L018 | COMPOSITE_RESEARCH_OR_DISPOSITION | INVENTORIED_COMPOSITE_UNIT_ATOMIC_SUPPORT_UNASSESSED | \| Handle \| Content \| Version pin \| Locators \| |
| R021:report:L020 | COMPOSITE_RESEARCH_OR_DISPOSITION | INVENTORIED_COMPOSITE_UNIT_ATOMIC_SUPPORT_UNASSESSED | \| S003 \| OME-Zarr 0.5 normative capture (Final Community Group Report, 8 September 2026; editor Josh Moore; OME, U. Dundee) \| `capture-only:5d8b240877ed2cf9596566187bcb3779ea011d5319807134c65e2e7dc2ae82de`; alias URI `https://ngff.openmicroscopy.org/0.5/` (catalog alias `SRC-2b11c54af373e1979af8cbb3`) \| lines 1–896; section map: §1 Storage 67–117; §1.2 HCS 118–148; §2 Metadata 149–808; §3 naming 809–812; §4 Implementations 813–814; §5 Citing 815–820; §6 Version history 821–866; Conformance 867–878 \| |
| R021:report:L022 | COMPOSITE_RESEARCH_OR_DISPOSITION | INVENTORIED_COMPOSITE_UNIT_ATOMIC_SUPPORT_UNASSESSED | Only S003 is admitted (catalog: “Fixed admitted source S003 only”). The Zarr v3 specification, OME-XML, UDUNITS-2, and the OMERO WebGateway documentation are referenced by S003 but not included; statements that rest on them are flagged as transfer limits (§8) rather than treated as verified. |
| R021:report:L026 | COMPOSITE_RESEARCH_OR_DISPOSITION | INVENTORIED_COMPOSITE_UNIT_ATOMIC_SUPPORT_UNASSESSED | **Group A — opening a fileset, listing/selecting images, navigating multiresolution data** |
| R021:report:L028 | COMPOSITE_RESEARCH_OR_DISPOSITION | INVENTORIED_COMPOSITE_UNIT_ATOMIC_SUPPORT_UNASSESSED | - **F01 Zarr v3 basis.** OME-Zarr 0.5 is implemented on Zarr specification v3; all Zarr features (codecs, chunk grids, chunk key encodings, data types, storage transformers) may appear unless explicitly disallowed (S003 67–72, 99–100). Reader codec breadth is a compatibility risk; chunk-aligned partial reads underpin responsiveness. |
| R021:report:L029 | COMPOSITE_RESEARCH_OR_DISPOSITION | INVENTORIED_COMPOSITE_UNIT_ATOMIC_SUPPORT_UNASSESSED | - **F02 Image hierarchy.** Each image is a Zarr group with `multiscales`/`omero` in `zarr.json` (87–89); pyramid levels are separate arrays whose names are arbitrary — ordering is defined by `multiscales` metadata (91–94); arrays are up to 5-D with time before channel before spatial axes (96–97); optional `labels` group sits beside the levels (102–108, 110–117). |
| R021:report:L030 | COMPOSITE_RESEARCH_OR_DISPOSITION | INVENTORIED_COMPOSITE_UNIT_ATOMIC_SUPPORT_UNASSESSED | - **F03 HCS hierarchy.** Plate/row/well grouping with MUST-level well and plate implementations (119–127); empty rows/wells SHOULD NOT be present, so sparseness is normal (128–129). |
| R021:report:L031 | COMPOSITE_RESEARCH_OR_DISPOSITION | INVENTORIED_COMPOSITE_UNIT_ATOMIC_SUPPORT_UNASSESSED | - **F04 `ome` namespace and version.** All OME metadata lives under `attributes.ome`; the version string MUST be consistent within a hierarchy (152–156). |
| R021:report:L032 | COMPOSITE_RESEARCH_OR_DISPOSITION | INVENTORIED_COMPOSITE_UNIT_ATOMIC_SUPPORT_UNASSESSED | - **F06 bioformats2raw.layout collections.** Transitional multi-image layout (`OME/METADATA.ome.xml`, `series`, numbered groups). MUSTs: layout value `3` (256); `series` is a string list whose order matches OME-XML Images (265–267); consecutive-numbered fallback (268–270); `plate` key takes precedence when present (204–206, 262). Readers SHOULD make users aware of more than one image and SHOULD NOT default to only the first (271–275). |
| R021:report:L033 | COMPOSITE_RESEARCH_OR_DISPOSITION | INVENTORIED_COMPOSITE_UNIT_ATOMIC_SUPPORT_UNASSESSED | - **F11 plate metadata.** MUST fields `columns`, `rows`, `version`, `wells` (with 0-based `rowIndex`/`columnIndex`, path = row + / + column) and acquisition `id` uniqueness; SHOULD `field_count`, plate `name` (518–560); dense and sparse examples (561–739). |
| R021:report:L034 | COMPOSITE_RESEARCH_OR_DISPOSITION | INVENTORIED_COMPOSITE_UNIT_ATOMIC_SUPPORT_UNASSESSED | - **F12 well metadata.** MUST `images` list of fields of view with unique alphanumeric case-sensitive paths; `acquisition` id required when the plate has multiple acquisitions (744–750). |
| R021:report:L035 | COMPOSITE_RESEARCH_OR_DISPOSITION | INVENTORIED_COMPOSITE_UNIT_ATOMIC_SUPPORT_UNASSESSED | - **F13 JSON strictness.** Comments MUST NOT appear in JSON objects (65–66); transitional metadata may be MUST/SHOULD to read (60–64); camelCase convention with legacy exceptions (809–812). |
| R021:report:L036 | COMPOSITE_RESEARCH_OR_DISPOSITION | INVENTORIED_COMPOSITE_UNIT_ATOMIC_SUPPORT_UNASSESSED | - **F14 Conformance and currency.** RFC 2119 keywords; all non-example text normative (56–59, 867–878); edition pinned to 0.5 (8 Sep 2026); editor’s drafts not necessarily supported (24–27); 0.5.0 moved to Zarr v3 (831–833), so 0.4-era (v2) filesets are outside a pure v3 reader; Implementations section is only a pointer (813–814). |
| R021:report:L038 | COMPOSITE_RESEARCH_OR_DISPOSITION | INVENTORIED_COMPOSITE_UNIT_ATOMIC_SUPPORT_UNASSESSED | **Group B — channel/time/plane display, coordinate details, label overlays** |
| R021:report:L040 | COMPOSITE_RESEARCH_OR_DISPOSITION | INVENTORIED_COMPOSITE_UNIT_ATOMIC_SUPPORT_UNASSESSED | - **F05 axes.** `name` MUST exist and be unique; `type` and `unit` are SHOULD-level (types `space`/`time`/`channel`, custom allowed; UDUNITS-2 strings enumerated at 171–172); axes length MUST equal array dimensionality; `dimension_names` MUST be included in each level array’s `zarr.json` and match (167–174). |
| R021:report:L041 | COMPOSITE_RESEARCH_OR_DISPOSITION | INVENTORIED_COMPOSITE_UNIT_ATOMIC_SUPPORT_UNASSESSED | - **F07 coordinateTransformations.** Entries MUST have `type` ∈ {`identity` (default), `translation`, `scale`}; translation/scale vectors may be inline or binary via `path`; applied sequentially in list order (276–293). |
| R021:report:L042 | COMPOSITE_RESEARCH_OR_DISPOSITION | QUALIFICATION_REQUIRED | - **F08 multiscales.** MUSTs: `axes` (2–5 dims, time→channel/custom→space order, 299–302); `datasets` ordered largest-to-smallest resolution (304–306); per-dataset transforms with exactly one `scale` (default 1.0 per axis when no downsampling), optional single `translation` after `scale`, vector lengths equal to axes (308–312); SHOULDs: `zyx` spatial order (303), `name`/`type`/`metadata` (317–319); MAY: group-level transforms applied after dataset-level ones (314–316); multiple named multiscales: user chooses by name, first is fallback (388–397). |
| R021:report:L043 | COMPOSITE_RESEARCH_OR_DISPOSITION | INVENTORIED_COMPOSITE_UNIT_ATOMIC_SUPPORT_UNASSESSED | - **F09 omero (transitional).** Optional; if present MUST contain `channels`, each with 6-hex-digit `color` and `window` (`min`/`max`/`start`/`end`) (426–430); `rdefs` defaults (`defaultT`, `defaultZ`, `model`) appear in the informative example only (401–425). |
| R021:report:L044 | COMPOSITE_RESEARCH_OR_DISPOSITION | INVENTORIED_COMPOSITE_UNIT_ATOMIC_SUPPORT_UNASSESSED | - **F10 labels/image-label.** Label pixels MUST be integer dtypes (438–439); the `labels` group MUST list member paths in `zarr.json` (441–443, listing all is SHOULD; cf. prose at 104–105); label images MUST implement multiscales with the same number of levels as the source image (454–455); `image-label` SHOULD with `colors` (MUST array; per-object integer `label-value`; MAY `rgba` 0–255 with alpha) — readers SHOULD display with those colors (456–466); optional `properties` and `source` (default `../../`) (467–474); label dims equal image dims or 1 (106–108). |
| R021:report:L048 | COMPOSITE_RESEARCH_OR_DISPOSITION | INVENTORIED_COMPOSITE_UNIT_ATOMIC_SUPPORT_UNASSESSED | \| Class \| Items (source lines) \| |
| R021:report:L050 | COMPOSITE_RESEARCH_OR_DISPOSITION | INVENTORIED_COMPOSITE_UNIT_ATOMIC_SUPPORT_UNASSESSED | \| Established obligations (MUST) \| Zarr v3 basis for 0.5 (68–69); `ome` version consistency (156); axes unique `name`, length = dims, `dimension_names` present/matching (168, 173–174); multiscales axes/datasets presence, 2–5 dims, type order (299–302); dataset path order largest→smallest (305–306); exactly one scale per dataset, translation after scale, vector lengths (308–312); transform types restricted in multiscales context (309); transform entry `type` field (279–280); sequential application (293); labels integer dtypes (438–439), `labels` key listing (441–443), label multiscales with equal level count (454–455), intermediate label groups carry no metadata (439–440); b2fr layout value `3` (256), series string list + order rules (265–267), numbered-group fallback (268–270), plate precedence (204–206); plate `columns`/`rows`/`version`/`wells` constraints (530–560); well `images` constraints and conditional `acquisition` (744–750); conditional omero `channels`/`color`/`window` fields (426–430); no JSON comments (65–66) \| |
| R021:report:L051 | COMPOSITE_RESEARCH_OR_DISPOSITION | INVENTORIED_COMPOSITE_UNIT_ATOMIC_SUPPORT_UNASSESSED | \| Optional capabilities (SHOULD/MAY in the format) \| axes `type`/`unit` present (169–170); `zyx` ordering (303); multiscales `name`/`type`/`metadata` (317–319); group-level coordinateTransformations (314–316); translation at all (311); omero presence (426); image-label and rgba colors (456–466); label listing completeness (442); plate `field_count`/`name`, acquisition names (523–541); well `version` (751–752); binary `path` form of transforms (285–289); reader MAY show all images or offer a choice, MAY use `series`, MAY ignore unrelated groups (273–275); multiple named multiscales chooser with first-as-fallback (388–397) \| |
| R021:report:L052 | COMPOSITE_RESEARCH_OR_DISPOSITION | INVENTORIED_COMPOSITE_UNIT_ATOMIC_SUPPORT_UNASSESSED | \| Product decisions (format silent or open) \| Codec support breadth (F01); HTTP/S3 backends — out of product scope (73–77); presentation of multi-image collections (274); default rendering when `omero` absent (F09); label color fallback when `image-label` absent (F10); unit display formatting and any conversion (F05); rejection vs best-effort on version inconsistency (F04); handling of non-conforming inputs and error-message design (format defines validity, not reader behavior); whether to attempt 0.4/Zarr-v2 filesets (F14); acquisition filtering UI in HCS (F11/F12); supplementing the `labels` list with tree scanning (F10) \| |
| R021:report:L056 | COMPOSITE_RESEARCH_OR_DISPOSITION | INVENTORIED_COMPOSITE_UNIT_ATOMIC_SUPPORT_UNASSESSED | - **Slice 01** (fixture disclaimer): no format obligations; research does not alter it. |
| R021:report:L057 | COMPOSITE_RESEARCH_OR_DISPOSITION | INVENTORIED_COMPOSITE_UNIT_ATOMIC_SUPPORT_UNASSESSED | - **Slice 02** (open local fileset, list images; canvas pan/zoom; channel visibility; time/plane selection; label overlays; details panel with dimensions/units/coordinates; automatic pyramid-level choice): |
| R021:report:L058 | COMPOSITE_RESEARCH_OR_DISPOSITION | INVENTORIED_COMPOSITE_UNIT_ATOMIC_SUPPORT_UNASSESSED |   - *Listing images* is normatively reinforced: readers SHOULD NOT default to only the first image in a bioformats2raw collection (272). Listing must be metadata-driven across three layouts: plain multiscale groups (F02), b2fr collections via `series` or numbered groups (F06), and plate→well→field traversal (F03, F11, F12). Disposition: keep the slice, add collection/HCS awareness the thin plan lacks. |
| R021:report:L059 | COMPOSITE_RESEARCH_OR_DISPOSITION | INVENTORIED_COMPOSITE_UNIT_ATOMIC_SUPPORT_UNASSESSED |   - *Pyramid-level choice* must read `datasets` order (largest first, 305–306) rather than array names (arbitrary, 93–94); with multiple named multiscales, follow the choose-by-name/first-fallback pattern (388–397). |
| R021:report:L060 | COMPOSITE_RESEARCH_OR_DISPOSITION | INVENTORIED_COMPOSITE_UNIT_ATOMIC_SUPPORT_UNASSESSED |   - *Channel/time/plane controls* classify axes by `type` with time→channel→space order (301–302), tolerating custom types (169) and absent units (170); `omero` channels/windows supply defaults when present (426–430) but fallbacks are needed (product decision). |
| R021:report:L061 | COMPOSITE_RESEARCH_OR_DISPOSITION | QUALIFICATION_REQUIRED |   - *Calibrated coordinates* compose dataset-level then group-level transforms in order (293, 308–316): physical position from mandatory `scale` (default 1.0) plus optional `translation`; identity default (283). A `path`-form transform reader is needed for full conformance (F07) or a documented limitation. |
| R021:report:L062 | COMPOSITE_RESEARCH_OR_DISPOSITION | INVENTORIED_COMPOSITE_UNIT_ATOMIC_SUPPORT_UNASSESSED |   - *Label overlays*: discover via `labels` list (441–443), guarantee of level-count parity enables level-aligned overlay (454–455), color per `colors[].rgba` with alpha (461–466); note unlisted-label risk (SHOULD-level listing). |
| R021:report:L063 | COMPOSITE_RESEARCH_OR_DISPOSITION | INVENTORIED_COMPOSITE_UNIT_ATOMIC_SUPPORT_UNASSESSED | - **Slice 03** (background reads; cancel stale work; explainable failures): format-neutral for threading/cancellation; the chunk-file model (99–100) supports chunk-aligned partial reads that keep navigation responsive. The format defines validity but no reader error behavior, so “understandable feedback” is product work — detectable non-conformances include JSON comments (65–66), version inconsistency (156), `dimension_names` mismatch (174), wrong vector lengths (312), non-integer labels (438–439), duplicate well paths (745–747), bad well path shape (553–556). |
| R021:report:L064 | COMPOSITE_RESEARCH_OR_DISPOSITION | QUALIFICATION_REQUIRED | - **Slice 04** (interoperate with real tools’ 0.5 filesets; read-only; extras out of scope): requires reading transitional metadata — b2fr layout and `omero` — because those capture real tool output “in the wild” (180–181, 60–64). Version envelope: 0.5 implies Zarr v3 (831–833); 0.4 filesets are a deliberate scope decision (F14). Read-only behavior conflicts with no reader obligation (the spec imposes reading duties only). |
| R021:report:L065 | PROPOSAL_OR_UNEXECUTED_TEST | INVENTORIED_COMPOSITE_UNIT_ATOMIC_SUPPORT_UNASSESSED | - **Slice 05** (acceptance on representative filesets; large dataset must not freeze; malformed input feedback): the representative set should include the layouts and negative cases enumerated in §10 (UNEXECUTED). |

| R022:report:L023 | COMPOSITE_RESEARCH_OR_DISPOSITION | INVENTORIED_COMPOSITE_UNIT_ATOMIC_SUPPORT_UNASSESSED | - The spec adopts RFC 2119 keywords (S003:L57–59). Its conformance section states the key words "do not appear in all uppercase letters in this specification" yet retain RFC 2119 force, and that all text is normative except examples, notes and explicitly non-normative sections (S003:L869–888). Quoted lowercase "must/should" is therefore normative. |
| R022:report:L024 | UNCERTAINTY_OR_NEGATIVE_SCOPE | INVENTORIED_COMPOSITE_UNIT_ATOMIC_SUPPORT_UNASSESSED | - Citations read **S003:L<lines>** (identity/version per §0.1). Labels: **[OBLIGATION]** (MUST/SHOULD-level requirement), **[OPTIONAL]** (MAY-level), **[CONDITION]** (holds only under stated circumstances), **[MECHANISM]**, **[FAILURE]**, **[UNRESOLVED]**, **[COUNTEREVIDENCE]**, **[INFERENCE]** (my conclusion, not a source statement). |
| R022:report:L025 | PROPOSAL_OR_UNEXECUTED_TEST | INVENTORIED_COMPOSITE_UNIT_ATOMIC_SUPPORT_UNASSESSED | - Negative claims are scoped to this capture only (896 lines, version 0.5, hash above). All proposed validation (§2.6) is **UNEXECUTED**. |
| R022:report:L033 | COMPOSITE_RESEARCH_OR_DISPOSITION | INVENTORIED_COMPOSITE_UNIT_ATOMIC_SUPPORT_UNASSESSED | **F-01 [OBLIGATION][CONDITION] Requirement language and normative scope.** RFC 2119 keywords govern; all text is normative except examples, notes and explicitly non-normative sections; requirement words appear in lower/upper case without loss of force. S003:L57–59, L869–888. Condition for every finding below: text inside examples (e.g., the JSON blocks at L320–387, L476–512, L561–640, L643–739, L756–784, L788–808) is illustrative unless a normative sentence states a rule. |
| R022:report:L035 | COMPOSITE_RESEARCH_OR_DISPOSITION | INVENTORIED_COMPOSITE_UNIT_ATOMIC_SUPPORT_UNASSESSED | **F-02 [OBLIGATION][MECHANISM] Zarr v3 substrate with a wide allowed-feature surface.** OME-Zarr is implemented on Zarr format version 3; "All features of the Zarr format including codecs, chunk grids, chunk key encodings, data types and storage transformers may be used with OME-Zarr unless explicitly disallowed in this specification" (L68–72). Version history: Zarr v3 adopted in 0.5.0 (2024-11-21, L831–833). Condition: the spec disallows almost nothing explicitly, so a conformant fileset may use any Zarr v3 codec/transformer — a reader supports a subset or must explain what it cannot display. [INFERENCE] The corpus does not enumerate which Zarr features real producers use (see F-31, F-32). |
| R022:report:L037 | COMPOSITE_RESEARCH_OR_DISPOSITION | INVENTORIED_COMPOSITE_UNIT_ATOMIC_SUPPORT_UNASSESSED | **F-03 [OBLIGATION] Metadata substrate and version consistency.** OME-Zarr metadata lives in the various `zarr.json` files under the namespaced `ome` key in attributes; the metadata version is a string in `ome.version` (example "0.5", L152–165); "The OME-Zarr Metadata version MUST be consistent within a hierarchy" (L155–156). [FAILURE] Inconsistent `ome.version` across a hierarchy violates the spec; detection is a reader-side validation duty (no format-defined behavior, see F-30). |
| R022:report:L039 | COMPOSITE_RESEARCH_OR_DISPOSITION | INVENTORIED_COMPOSITE_UNIT_ATOMIC_SUPPORT_UNASSESSED | **F-04 [OBLIGATION][FAILURE] JSON comments are prohibited in data.** "comments MUST NOT be included in JSON objects"; comments in the document's examples are for clarity only (L65–66). [COUNTEREVIDENCE/tension] The spec's own examples embed `//` comments (L341, L351, L361, L370; also the annotated `omero` block L401–423), so example blocks are not valid JSON as printed — copying them verbatim produces non-conformant metadata (F-33). |
| R022:report:L041 | COMPOSITE_RESEARCH_OR_DISPOSITION | INVENTORIED_COMPOSITE_UNIT_ATOMIC_SUPPORT_UNASSESSED | **F-05 [MECHANISM] Storage neutrality.** The hierarchy "is represented here as it would appear locally but could equally be stored on a web server to be accessed via HTTP or in object storage like S3 or GCS" (L73–77). Condition for Slide Scout: the format imposes no local-filesystem assumption, so a local-only reader is a product subset, not a format accommodation. |
| R022:report:L045 | COMPOSITE_RESEARCH_OR_DISPOSITION | INVENTORIED_COMPOSITE_UNIT_ATOMIC_SUPPORT_UNASSESSED | **F-06 [OBLIGATION][MECHANISM] Image fileset layout.** One image = one Zarr group; group-level attributes in `zarr.json` include "multiscales" and "omero" (L87–89). Each multiscale level is a separate Zarr array; "The name of the array is arbitrary with the ordering defined by the 'multiscales' metadata, but is often a sequence starting at 0" (L91–94). "All image arrays must be up to 5-dimensional with the axis of type time before type channel, before spatial axes" (L96–97). Chunks conform to the Zarr array specification and the array's own `zarr.json` (L99–100). Dimensionality is variable between 2 and 5 and "axis names are arbitrary" (L81). [FAILURE] Discovery or level-ordering logic that hardcodes names ("0","1","2") or axis names is unsupported by the format except in the numbered bioformats2raw collection case (F-24, L268–269). |
| R022:report:L047 | COMPOSITE_RESEARCH_OR_DISPOSITION | INVENTORIED_COMPOSITE_UNIT_ATOMIC_SUPPORT_UNASSESSED | **F-07 [OBLIGATION][CONDITION] Labels placement and pixel constraints.** A `labels` group nested in the image group holds label images; its `zarr.json` lists them, e.g. `{ "labels": [ "original/0" ] }` (L102–105). Each label dimension "should" equal the corresponding image dimension or be 1 if irrelevant (L106–108, lowercase should → normative per F-01). Intermediate folders are permitted but "currently contain no extra metadata" (L110). Label multiscales carry "only integer values" (L116–117); normatively, label pixels MUST be one of [uint8, int8, uint16, int16, uint32, int32, uint64, int64] (L438–439), and intermediate groups between `labels` and its images "MUST NOT contain metadata" (L439–440). [FAILURE] Float label data or metadata-bearing intermediate groups are non-conformant. |
| R022:report:L049 | COMPOSITE_RESEARCH_OR_DISPOSITION | INVENTORIED_COMPOSITE_UNIT_ATOMIC_SUPPORT_UNASSESSED | **F-08 [OBLIGATION][CONDITION] High-content screening (HCS) hierarchy.** For HCS datasets "Three groups MUST be defined above the images": the group above the images is the well and MUST implement the well specification; above it a row; above that a plate which MUST implement the plate specification (L119–127). A well row group SHOULD NOT be present if there are no images in the row; a well group SHOULD NOT be present if there are no images in the well (L128–129). Condition: these obligations govern HCS filesets; the spec does not state that every OME-Zarr fileset is HCS. |
| R022:report:L053 | COMPOSITE_RESEARCH_OR_DISPOSITION | QUALIFICATION_REQUIRED | **F-09 [OBLIGATION][OPTIONAL] `axes`.** Each axis dict MUST contain `name`, unique across all names (L167–168); SHOULD contain `type`, SHOULD be "space"/"time"/"channel", MAY be another string for custom axis types (L169); SHOULD contain `unit`, the value SHOULD be one of the enumerated UDUNITS-2 strings (space units L171, time units L172). Length of `axes` MUST equal the dimensionality of the image arrays (L173). Conditions: names, types and units are only SHOULD-level except name presence/uniqueness and the length rule — a conformant file may omit units or use custom axis types. |
| R022:report:L055 | COMPOSITE_RESEARCH_OR_DISPOSITION | INVENTORIED_COMPOSITE_UNIT_ATOMIC_SUPPORT_UNASSESSED | **F-10 [OBLIGATION] `dimension_names` on arrays.** "The 'dimension_names' attribute MUST be included in the zarr.json of the Zarr array of a multiscale level and MUST match the names in the 'axes' metadata" (L174). Version qualification: this MUST was clarified in 0.5.2 (2025-01-10, L825–827). Note the locus difference: `axes` is group-level metadata; `dimension_names` is array-level — a validating reader must check both. |
| R022:report:L057 | COMPOSITE_RESEARCH_OR_DISPOSITION | INVENTORIED_COMPOSITE_UNIT_ATOMIC_SUPPORT_UNASSESSED | **F-11 [OBLIGATION][CONDITION] `multiscales` axes constraints and ordering.** Each multiscales dict MUST contain `axes` (L299); length must be 2–5 and equal to array dimensionality (L300); MUST contain 2 or 3 `space` entries, MAY contain one `time` entry, and MAY contain one additional `channel` **or** null/custom-type entry (L301 — i.e., at most five axes, with a single second non-space slot). Entry order MUST correspond to array dimension order and MUST be ordered by type: time first (if present), then channel/custom (if present), then space (L302); if there are three spatial axes (zyx stacking), spatial axes SHOULD be ordered "zyx" (L303). Image = 2–5 dimensional data with optional time/channel axes, stored as a multiple-resolution representation (L296–297). [FAILURE] Axis-order violations make physical-coordinate mapping ill-posed. |
| R022:report:L059 | COMPOSITE_RESEARCH_OR_DISPOSITION | INVENTORIED_COMPOSITE_UNIT_ATOMIC_SUPPORT_UNASSESSED | **F-12 [OBLIGATION] `datasets` (resolution levels).** Each multiscales dict MUST contain `datasets`, a list of dicts; each MUST contain `path` relative to the current zarr group; "The 'path's MUST be ordered from largest (i.e. highest resolution) to smallest" (L304–306). All dataset dicts MUST have the same number of dimensions, MUST NOT exceed 5, and number/order of dimensions MUST correspond to `axes` (L307). [MECHANISM] This ordering is what lets a viewer pick a pyramid level without inspecting shapes first. |
| R022:report:L061 | COMPOSITE_RESEARCH_OR_DISPOSITION | SOURCE_SUPPORTED_WITH_CONDITIONS | **F-13 [OBLIGATION][CONDITION] Dataset `coordinateTransformations` (calibration core).** Each dataset MUST contain `coordinateTransformations` mapping data coordinates to physical coordinates for that level; transformations MUST only be of type translation or scale (L308–309). They "MUST contain exactly one scale transformation that specifies the pixel size in physical units or time duration"; if scaling information is unavailable or inapplicable for an axis, the value MUST express the scaling factor between the current and the first resolution for that axis, defaulting to 1.0 without downsampling (L310). It MAY contain exactly one translation, which MUST be listed after scale (so it is expressed in physical coordinates); scale/translation array lengths MUST equal the length of `axes` (L311–312). Rationale: a simple data→physical mapping compatible with the general transformation spec (L313). Conditions: the relative-factor default applies only when absolute scaling is unavailable for an axis. |
| R022:report:L063 | COMPOSITE_RESEARCH_OR_DISPOSITION | INVENTORIED_COMPOSITE_UNIT_ATOMIC_SUPPORT_UNASSESSED | **F-14 [OPTIONAL][MECHANISM] Multiscales-level `coordinateTransformations`.** A multiscales dict MAY contain group-level transformations applied to all levels, after the dataset-level ones, under the same type/order rules; e.g., to give a scale for a dimension shared by all resolutions (L314–316; example: time unit 0.1 ms at L368–374). Transformations in a list "are applied sequentially and in order" (L293). [INFERENCE] Correct physical coordinates therefore require composing dataset-level then multiscales-level transforms in list order, with `identity` as the default transform (L282–283). |
| R022:report:L065 | COMPOSITE_RESEARCH_OR_DISPOSITION | INVENTORIED_COMPOSITE_UNIT_ATOMIC_SUPPORT_UNASSESSED | **F-15 [OPTIONAL][MECHANISM][FAILURE] Transform vectors may be external binary data.** For translation and scale, the vector is "one of: `translation`/`scale`:List[float], `path`:str — stored either as a list of floats or as binary data at a location in this container"; vector length defines dimensionality (L284–292). Condition: a conformant 0.5 file may legally store its scale vector at a binary path rather than inline. [FAILURE] A reader that only accepts inline float lists will fail on conformant files unless it detects the `path` form and explains or handles it. |
| R022:report:L067 | UNCERTAINTY_OR_NEGATIVE_SCOPE | QUALIFICATION_REQUIRED | **F-16 [CONDITION][UNRESOLVED] Multiple multiscales entries.** "If only one multiscale is provided, use it. Otherwise, the user can choose by name, using the first multiscale as a fallback" — followed by reader pseudocode including "Or perhaps choose based on chunk size" (L388–397). [UNRESOLVED] This passage sits in the normative body but is code-shaped; its exact conformance force (normative rule vs informative guidance) is not settled by the text. Observed content: name-based user choice with first-entry fallback. |
| R022:report:L069 | COMPOSITE_RESEARCH_OR_DISPOSITION | INVENTORIED_COMPOSITE_UNIT_ATOMIC_SUPPORT_UNASSESSED | **F-17 [OPTIONAL] Multiscales descriptive fields.** `name` SHOULD be present (L317); `type` (downscaling method) SHOULD be present (L318); `metadata` about the method SHOULD be present (L319; example L375–382). All optional. |
| R022:report:L071 | COMPOSITE_RESEARCH_OR_DISPOSITION | INVENTORIED_COMPOSITE_UNIT_ATOMIC_SUPPORT_UNASSESSED | **F-18 [OPTIONAL][CONDITION] `omero` render metadata (transitional).** Transitional channel/render information under the `omero` key: `id`, `name`, `channels` (annotated in the example as "Array matching the c dimension size", L403), and `rdefs` with `defaultT`, `defaultZ`, `model` "color"/"greyscale" (L398–423). "The 'omero' metadata is optional, but if present it MUST contain the field 'channels'"; each channel dict MUST contain `color` (6 hex digits, RGB) and `window`; `window` MUST contain `min`,`max`,`start`,`end` (L426–430). Semantic reference is external: "See the OMERO WebGateway documentation" (L424–425). Transitional rule: implementations may be expected (MUST) or encouraged (SHOULD) to support **reading** transitional metadata; writing is usually optional (MAY) (L60–64). [CONDITION] All channel colors/windows/rendering defaults exist only when `omero` is present. |
| R022:report:L073 | COMPOSITE_RESEARCH_OR_DISPOSITION | SOURCE_SUPPORTED_LIMITATION | **F-19 [OBLIGATION][CONDITION] Labels listing and label-image parity.** The labels group's `zarr.json` MUST contain a `labels` key whose value is a JSON array of paths to the labeled multiscale image(s); all label images SHOULD be listed (L441–442). The label image's `zarr.json` MUST implement the multiscales specification, and its `datasets` array "MUST have the same number of entries (scale levels) as the original unlabeled image" (L454–455). [MECHANISM] This parity rule is what makes level-aligned overlays possible; it constrains level counts, not necessarily per-level shapes. |
| R022:report:L075 | UNCERTAINTY_OR_NEGATIVE_SCOPE | INVENTORIED_COMPOSITE_UNIT_ATOMIC_SUPPORT_UNASSESSED | **F-20 [OPTIONAL][OBLIGATION] `image-label` display metadata.** The label image SHOULD contain an `image-label` object, which SHOULD contain: `colors` (MUST be a JSON array; each object MUST contain integer `label-value`; MAY contain `rgba` = four integers 0–255, fourth = alpha/opacity) and `version` (MUST be a string, the image-label schema version) (L456–466). "Conforming readers SHOULD display labels using the colors specified" (L461). `image-label` MAY contain `properties` (array; each object MUST contain `label-value`, plus arbitrary key-value pairs which need not be consistent across entries) and `source` (MUST be an object; MAY include `image`, a relative path to the source image group, default "../../") (L467–474; example L476–512; display interpretation L513–514). [UNRESOLVED] The set of valid `version` strings is not enumerated in the capture. |
| R022:report:L077 | COMPOSITE_RESEARCH_OR_DISPOSITION | INVENTORIED_COMPOSITE_UNIT_ATOMIC_SUPPORT_UNASSESSED | **F-21 [OBLIGATION] Label/image dimension correspondence (should-level).** Each label dimension should equal the corresponding image dimension or be 1 if irrelevant (L106–108). Weaker than F-19's MUST parity of level counts; overlay code should validate rather than assume identical shapes. |
| R022:report:L079 | COMPOSITE_RESEARCH_OR_DISPOSITION | INVENTORIED_COMPOSITE_UNIT_ATOMIC_SUPPORT_UNASSESSED | **F-22 [OBLIGATION][OPTIONAL] `plate` metadata.** MUST contain `columns` and `rows` (every physical row/column MUST be defined even if it has no wells; each name: string, alphanumeric only, case-sensitive, MUST NOT duplicate; care SHOULD be taken to avoid case-insensitive-filesystem collisions such as "Aa"/"aA") (L530–549); MUST contain `version` (string) (L550–551); MUST contain `wells`: each well MUST have `path` = row name + "/" + column name in that order with no extra leading/trailing directories, MUST have 0-based `rowIndex`/`columnIndex`, and path+indices MUST all refer to the same row/column pair (L552–560). SHOULD contain `field_count` ("positive integer defining the maximum number of fields per view across all wells" — the spec's phrasing, L538–539) and `name` (L540–541). MAY contain `acquisitions`: each MUST have unique integer `id` ≥ 0 within the plate; SHOULD have `name`, `maximumfieldcount` (positive integer); MAY have `description`, `starttime`/`endtime` (integer epoch timestamps) (L518–529). Examples: 2×3 plate with two acquisitions (L561–640); sparse 96-well plate with 2 wells but complete 12×8 row/column lists (L641–739). |
| R022:report:L081 | COMPOSITE_RESEARCH_OR_DISPOSITION | INVENTORIED_COMPOSITE_UNIT_ATOMIC_SUPPORT_UNASSESSED | **F-23 [OBLIGATION][CONDITION] `well` metadata.** MUST contain `images`: a list of all fields of view; each MUST have `path` (alphanumeric, case-sensitive, unique within the list) (L744–748). [CONDITION] If multiple acquisitions were performed, each image MUST contain an integer `acquisition` key matching one of the plate's acquisitions (L748–750). The well dict SHOULD contain `version` whose value MUST be a string (L751–752). Examples L753–808. |
| R022:report:L083 | COMPOSITE_RESEARCH_OR_DISPOSITION | INVENTORIED_COMPOSITE_UNIT_ATOMIC_SUPPORT_UNASSESSED | **F-24 [OBLIGATION][CONDITION] `bioformats2raw.layout` collections (transitional).** Added in v0.4 for filesets "that already exist in the wild"; "An upcoming NGFF specification will replace this layout" (L180–181). Conforming groups MUST have value "3" for the key in the top-level `zarr.json` (L256); SHOULD have `OME/METADATA.ome.xml` (see F-26); the OME group MAY contain a `series` attribute that MUST be a list of string paths whose order MUST match the order of `Image` elements in the OME-XML if provided (L265–267). Discovery logic: if `plate` metadata is present, images MUST be located at the defined plate positions; if no `series` and no plate, multiscales images MUST be stored in consecutively numbered groups starting from 0 ("0/","1/","2/",…); every multiscales group MUST represent exactly one OME-XML Image in the same order as the series index or group numbers (L261–270). |
| R022:report:L085 | COMPOSITE_RESEARCH_OR_DISPOSITION | INVENTORIED_COMPOSITE_UNIT_ATOMIC_SUPPORT_UNASSESSED | **F-25 [OBLIGATION][CONDITION] Plate precedence; no mixing.** If the top-level group is a plate, `bioformats2raw.layout` may be present but the `plate` key MUST also be present, takes precedence, and parsing should follow the plate specification; "It is not possible to mix collections of images with plates at present" (L204–206). |
| R022:report:L087 | COMPOSITE_RESEARCH_OR_DISPOSITION | INVENTORIED_COMPOSITE_UNIT_ATOMIC_SUPPORT_UNASSESSED | **F-26 [OBLIGATION][CONDITION] OME-XML sidecar.** `OME/METADATA.ome.xml` (when provided): MUST adhere to the OME-XML specification and MUST use `<MetadataOnly/>` as opposed to `<BinData/>`, `<BinaryOnly/>` or `<TiffData/>`; MAY use the minimum specification (L257–260). |
| R022:report:L089 | COMPOSITE_RESEARCH_OR_DISPOSITION | INVENTORIED_COMPOSITE_UNIT_ATOMIC_SUPPORT_UNASSESSED | **F-27 [OBLIGATION] Reader duties for multi-image collections.** Conforming readers SHOULD make users aware of the presence of more than one image (i.e., SHOULD NOT default to only opening the first); MAY use `series` to determine valid groups; MAY show all images or offer a choice (as with HCS plates); MAY ignore other groups/arrays under the root (L271–275). |
| R022:report:L091 | COMPOSITE_RESEARCH_OR_DISPOSITION | INVENTORIED_COMPOSITE_UNIT_ATOMIC_SUPPORT_UNASSESSED | **F-28 [OBLIGATION][FAILURE] Key-spelling compatibility.** Multi-word keys should use camelCase, "but some parts of the specification don't obey this convention as they were added before this was adopted, but they should be updated in due course" (L809–812). Live example: the plate acquisitions field is specified as `maximumfieldcount` (all lowercase, L524) while wells use camelCase `rowIndex`/`columnIndex` (L557–559). [FAILURE] Parsers must use each key's specified spelling exactly, and the spec itself flags future renames — key sets are version-pinned. |
| R022:report:L093 | COMPOSITE_RESEARCH_OR_DISPOSITION | INVENTORIED_COMPOSITE_UNIT_ATOMIC_SUPPORT_UNASSESSED | **F-29 [OBLIGATION] Versioning posture.** The current released version is 0.5; "Migration scripts will be provided between numbered versions. Data written with these latest changes (an 'editor's draft') will not necessarily be supported" (L25–27). Plate `version` MUST be a string (L550–551); well `version` SHOULD be present with string value (L751–752); `ome.version` MUST be consistent within a hierarchy (F-03). Version history L821–866 (0.5.2 dimension_names clarification L825–827; 0.5.0 Zarr v3 L831–833; 0.2.0 chunk dimension separator changed to "/" L849–851). |
| R022:report:L097 | COMPOSITE_RESEARCH_OR_DISPOSITION | INVENTORIED_COMPOSITE_UNIT_ATOMIC_SUPPORT_UNASSESSED | - **M-1 Multiresolution storage**: per-level Zarr arrays referenced by `datasets[].path`, ordered largest→smallest (F-12); level selection is reader-side (F-16). |
| R022:report:L098 | COMPOSITE_RESEARCH_OR_DISPOSITION | INVENTORIED_COMPOSITE_UNIT_ATOMIC_SUPPORT_UNASSESSED | - **M-2 Data→physical coordinate mapping**: ordered scale/translation composition with identity default, relative-scale fallback 1.0, optional group-level transforms applied after dataset-level ones (F-13, F-14, L282–293). |
| R022:report:L099 | COMPOSITE_RESEARCH_OR_DISPOSITION | INVENTORIED_COMPOSITE_UNIT_ATOMIC_SUPPORT_UNASSESSED | - **M-3 Image discovery**: branch on plate key (precedence, F-25) → bioformats2raw.layout + series/numbered groups (F-24) → labels listing for overlays (F-19); axis/array names arbitrary (F-06). |
| R022:report:L100 | COMPOSITE_RESEARCH_OR_DISPOSITION | INVENTORIED_COMPOSITE_UNIT_ATOMIC_SUPPORT_UNASSESSED | - **M-4 Chunked random access**: arrays are folders of chunk files conforming to the array's `zarr.json` (L91–100); this is the enabling mechanism for background reads and cancellation, but chunk key encoding/codec semantics live in the Zarr v3 specification, which is not in the capture (F-32). |
| R022:report:L104 | UNCERTAINTY_OR_NEGATIVE_SCOPE | SOURCE_SUPPORTED_NEGATIVE_SCOPE | **F-30 [FAILURE][UNRESOLVED — scoped negative] No error-handling semantics are defined.** Within this capture (TOC L28–47; full text read), the spec defines no error, recovery, warning or validation-reporting behavior for non-conformant filesets. Obligations are declarative; the only localized fallbacks are: identity as default transformation (L282–283); relative scale factor default 1.0 (L310); plate-over-b2f2raw precedence (L204–205); multiscale selection fallback (L388–397, force unresolved, F-16). [INFERENCE] Everything the thin plan promises about failure explanation is a reader/product responsibility, not a format guarantee; and there is no format-sanctioned way to distinguish "invalid" from "valid but unsupported" — a viewer must define that boundary itself. |
| R022:report:L106 | COMPOSITE_RESEARCH_OR_DISPOSITION | INVENTORIED_COMPOSITE_UNIT_ATOMIC_SUPPORT_UNASSESSED | **Exceptions and conditions that must stay attached to claims** (per R01): omero and bioformats2raw.layout are transitional — read support MAY be REQUIRED, write support is usually optional (L60–64, L175–176, L398–399); units and types on axes are SHOULD (F-09); custom axis types are MAY (L169, L301); translation is MAY with order condition (F-13); multiscales-level transforms are MAY (F-14); well `acquisition` keys are conditionally MUST only with multiple acquisitions (F-23); series ordering matches OME-XML only "if provided" (L267); label listing SHOULD include all label images (L442); image-label and its subkeys are SHOULD/MAY (F-20); empty rows/wells SHOULD NOT be present while rows/columns lists MUST still be complete (F-08, F-22). |
| R022:report:L110 | COMPOSITE_RESEARCH_OR_DISPOSITION | INVENTORIED_COMPOSITE_UNIT_ATOMIC_SUPPORT_UNASSESSED | - **C-1 [COUNTEREVIDENCE] 0.5/Zarr-v3 requirement vs wild legacy data.** 0.5 mandates Zarr v3 (F-02), yet the same specification retains transitional machinery precisely because layouts "already exist in the wild" (L180–181) and transitional read expectations exist (L60–64). [INFERENCE] A viewer claiming "OME-Zarr 0.5 interop" will still plausibly encounter 0.4-era structures (v2-style data, series/METADATA.ome.xml collections); whether those are in scope is a product decision the capture cannot settle. This is the strongest internal counterevidence to a v3-only reader assumption. |
| R022:report:L111 | COMPOSITE_RESEARCH_OR_DISPOSITION | INVENTORIED_COMPOSITE_UNIT_ATOMIC_SUPPORT_UNASSESSED | - **C-2 [COUNTEREVIDENCE] Examples vs JSON rule** — F-04/F-33: data MUST NOT contain comments (L65–66) while the spec's own examples do (L341, L351, L361, L370, L401–423). Practical trap for test fixtures built by copying examples. |

(L813–814). The brief asks for research on "actual reader implementations"; this cannot be satisfied from the admitted capture. Scoped claim: no reader-implementation evidence exists in this corpus (not: none exists anywhere). |

| R022:report:L126 | COMPOSITE_RESEARCH_OR_DISPOSITION | INVENTORIED_COMPOSITE_UNIT_ATOMIC_SUPPORT_UNASSESSED | Plan under review: inputs/plan/Viewer.md (thin fixture): open local OME-Zarr 0.5 fileset, list images, pan/zoom canvas, channel visibility, time-point/plane selection "where relevant", optional label overlays, details panel (dimensions/units/coordinates), automatic pyramid-level choice, background reads with cancellation, failure explanations, interop with real 0.5 tools, local-only read-only boundary, acceptance via representative filesets. |
| R022:report:L130 | COMPOSITE_RESEARCH_OR_DISPOSITION | INVENTORIED_COMPOSITE_UNIT_ATOMIC_SUPPORT_UNASSESSED | - **P-01 "opens a local fileset and lists the images it finds"** — Evidence: F-02, F-05, F-06, F-22, F-23, F-24, F-25, F-27. Implication: "the images it finds" requires a discovery procedure with at least three branches — (a) plain image groups with `multiscales`; (b) bioformats2raw collections (value "3", optional `series`, else consecutively numbered groups from 0, plate precedence if `plate` present); (c) HCS plate → row → well → fields. Readers SHOULD surface all images rather than default to the first (F-27). Gap: the plan does not mention collections or HCS at all. Disposition: add an explicit discovery algorithm; decide product scope for HCS (at minimum detect-and-explain unsupported plates, per the brief's "clearly explain data it cannot display"). Product decision: is pre-0.5/transitional wild data in scope (C-1)? |
| R022:report:L131 | COMPOSITE_RESEARCH_OR_DISPOSITION | INVENTORIED_COMPOSITE_UNIT_ATOMIC_SUPPORT_UNASSESSED | - **P-02 Canvas, channel visibility, time/plane selection "where relevant"** — Evidence: F-06, F-11, F-18, F-34, U-3. Implication: channel/time/plane structure is knowable from `axes` (which axes exist is itself optional: time and channel are MAY, F-11), but colors, windows and initial defaults (`defaultT`, `defaultZ`, `model`) exist only if the optional transitional `omero` metadata is present (F-18); window semantics are externally defined and not captured (U-3). Disposition: keep "where relevant" tied to axes presence; define an omero-absent rendering fallback (e.g., grayscale + data-derived range) as an explicit product decision — the format does not supply one. |
| R022:report:L132 | UNCERTAINTY_OR_NEGATIVE_SCOPE | INVENTORIED_COMPOSITE_UNIT_ATOMIC_SUPPORT_UNASSESSED | - **P-03 Details panel: dimensions, units, coordinates** — Evidence: F-09, F-11, F-13, F-14, F-15. Implication: dimensions come from axes/array shapes; units are SHOULD-level and may be absent or custom (F-09) — the panel needs a unitless/unknown presentation policy. "Correctly calibrated coordinates" requires implementing the composition pipeline: dataset-level transforms (exactly one scale; optional translation after scale) then multiscales-level transforms, in list order, identity default, relative-factor 1.0 fallback, lengths equal to axes count (F-13, F-14). Absolute positions need the translation offsets. Additionally, scale/translation vectors may be stored as `path` references to binary data (F-15): inline-only parsing will misread conformant files. Disposition: spec the transform pipeline; decide path-vector support (implement, or detect + explicit unsupported message). |
| R022:report:L133 | UNCERTAINTY_OR_NEGATIVE_SCOPE | INVENTORIED_COMPOSITE_UNIT_ATOMIC_SUPPORT_UNASSESSED | - **P-04 "The viewer chooses an available pyramid level"** — Evidence: F-12, F-16, F-06. Implication: the format constrains only the level list and its largest→smallest order; the selection heuristic is unconstrained (the only hint — chunk-size-based choice — is an informative code comment, L396, inside the force-unresolved passage F-16). With multiple multiscales entries, name-based user choice with first-entry fallback (F-16). Level names/paths are arbitrary — never hardcode (F-06). Disposition: fine as a plan; document the heuristic as a product choice and the multiple-multiscales UX. |
| R022:report:L134 | COMPOSITE_RESEARCH_OR_DISPOSITION | SOURCE_SUPPORTED_PROPOSAL | - **P-05 Optional label overlays** — Evidence: F-07, F-19, F-20, F-21. Implication: discovery via the `labels` listing (MUST exist when a labels group exists); level-aligned overlay is supported by the MUST parity of scale-level counts (F-19) plus the weaker should-level dimension correspondence (F-21) — validate at read time, do not assume identical shapes. Display SHOULD follow `image-label.colors` rgba incl. alpha (F-20); properties/source are optional extras. Label pixels are integer-only (F-07). Disposition: add read-time alignment validation, dtype check, and a color-missing fallback policy (product decision). |
| R022:report:L135 | COMPOSITE_RESEARCH_OR_DISPOSITION | INVENTORIED_COMPOSITE_UNIT_ATOMIC_SUPPORT_UNASSESSED | - **P-06 "Large image reads run in the background… cancels work"** — Evidence: F-02, F-05, F-06, M-4, U-2. Implication: the format's chunked array storage is the enabling mechanism, but chunk key encoding and codec semantics live in the uncaptured Zarr v3 spec (U-2); transfer limit — this corpus cannot verify read-granularity or decode-cost guarantees. Cancellation policy is pure engineering with no format constraints. Disposition: keep; validate empirically (V-4) and declare the supported codec subset with clear unsupported feedback (F-02). |
| R022:report:L136 | COMPOSITE_RESEARCH_OR_DISPOSITION | INVENTORIED_COMPOSITE_UNIT_ATOMIC_SUPPORT_UNASSESSED | - **P-07 "Opening failures explain which image or data could not be displayed"** — Evidence: F-30, F-03, F-04, F-10, F-28, plus the MUST checklist F-09/F-11/F-12/F-13/F-19/F-22/F-23. Implication: the format defines no error semantics (scoped negative, F-30), so the plan's promise is a product obligation. The validation checklist is derivable from the MUSTs: ome.version consistency (F-03), comment-free JSON (F-04), axes/dimension_names cross-check (F-09, F-10), axis order (F-11), dataset paths/order/dim counts (F-12), transform rules (F-13/F-14), labels listing and parity (F-19), plate/well cross-consistency — path↔row/column agreement (L552–560), acquisition matching (L748–750) — and exact key spellings such as `maximumfieldcount` (F-28). Disposition: add a validation-to-message mapping to the plan. |
| R022:report:L137 | COMPOSITE_RESEARCH_OR_DISPOSITION | INVENTORIED_COMPOSITE_UNIT_ATOMIC_SUPPORT_UNASSESSED | - **P-08 "Interoperate with filesets from real OME-Zarr 0.5 tools"; local-only; sources unchanged** — Evidence: F-02, F-05, F-24, F-28, F-29, F-31, C-1, U-1. Implication: the reader-library landscape is not observable in this corpus (F-31/U-1); transitional keys and spelling quirks are expected in real data (F-24, F-28); editor's-draft data is explicitly not necessarily supported by the spec itself (F-29). Read-only/local fits the format's storage neutrality (F-05). Disposition: pin the target to spec version 0.5.x with a documented stance on transitional/legacy structures; treat "real tools" claims as unverified until representative filesets are admitted (V-5, blocked). |
| R022:report:L141 | COMPOSITE_RESEARCH_OR_DISPOSITION | INVENTORIED_COMPOSITE_UNIT_ATOMIC_SUPPORT_UNASSESSED | - **A-1 Embed an existing OME-Zarr/Zarr reader library vs. hand-rolled minimal reader.** Option only: the capture's Implementations section is empty ("See Tools.", F-31), so any library's supported-version/feature matrix is unverified here and no live check is permitted. Transfer limit: this corpus provides zero evidence about which libraries exist, what they support, or their failure behavior. Decision requires evidence outside this assignment. |
| R022:report:L142 | COMPOSITE_RESEARCH_OR_DISPOSITION | INVENTORIED_COMPOSITE_UNIT_ATOMIC_SUPPORT_UNASSESSED | - **A-2 Pyramid-level selection heuristic (screen-size vs chunk-size vs full-image).** No normative constraint (F-16); the chunk-size hint comes from informative pseudocode. Transfer limit: heuristics tuned for 2D viewing may transfer poorly to 3D/4D/5D volumetric navigation; needs product tuning and measurement. |
| R022:report:L143 | COMPOSITE_RESEARCH_OR_DISPOSITION | INVENTORIED_COMPOSITE_UNIT_ATOMIC_SUPPORT_UNASSESSED | - **A-3 Transform handling: generic ordered composition incl. `path` vectors vs inline-only with detection.** Inline-only parsing fails on conformant files using path-stored vectors (F-15); at minimum, detect and explain. Transfer limit: binary vector layout details are not exemplified in the capture — implementation would need Zarr v3 array-reading capability (uncaptured, U-2). |
| R022:report:L144 | COMPOSITE_RESEARCH_OR_DISPOSITION | INVENTORIED_COMPOSITE_UNIT_ATOMIC_SUPPORT_UNASSESSED | - **A-4 HCS: full plate navigator vs detect-and-explain.** Plate/well metadata obligations are normative for HCS datasets (F-08, F-22, F-23), but supporting them is product scope; the sparse-plate example (L641–739) shows valid plates with few wells. Transfer limit: none from the format; the limit is product scope and the brief's requirement to explain non-displayed data. |
| R022:report:L145 | COMPOSITE_RESEARCH_OR_DISPOSITION | INVENTORIED_COMPOSITE_UNIT_ATOMIC_SUPPORT_UNASSESSED | - **A-5 Validation strictness: validate-then-open vs best-effort-open-with-warnings.** The format gives no guidance (F-30). Best-effort risks silently misapplying conditional rules (e.g., relative-scale fallback F-13, plate precedence F-25); strict validation better serves the plan's failure-explanation goal. Product decision. |
| R022:report:L149 | COMPOSITE_RESEARCH_OR_DISPOSITION | INVENTORIED_COMPOSITE_UNIT_ATOMIC_SUPPORT_UNASSESSED | - **X-01 Hardcoding level names ("0","1","2") or axis names for discovery/ordering** — rejected: names are arbitrary (F-06, L81, L93–94); only the bioformats2raw numbered-group convention is fixed, and only in its no-series/no-plate branch (F-24, L268–269). |
| R022:report:L150 | COMPOSITE_RESEARCH_OR_DISPOSITION | INVENTORIED_COMPOSITE_UNIT_ATOMIC_SUPPORT_UNASSESSED | - **X-02 Treating `omero` as guaranteed render metadata** — rejected: optional and transitional (F-18); a viewer must define omero-absent behavior. |
| R022:report:L151 | COMPOSITE_RESEARCH_OR_DISPOSITION | INVENTORIED_COMPOSITE_UNIT_ATOMIC_SUPPORT_UNASSESSED | - **X-03 Inline-only coordinateTransformations parsing without `path` detection** — rejected for conformance: path-stored vectors are spec-legal (F-15); at minimum the case must be detected and reported. |
| R022:report:L152 | COMPOSITE_RESEARCH_OR_DISPOSITION | INVENTORIED_COMPOSITE_UNIT_ATOMIC_SUPPORT_UNASSESSED | - **X-04 Defaulting to the first image of a multi-image collection** — rejected: readers SHOULD NOT default to only the first image (F-27, L272). |
| R022:report:L153 | COMPOSITE_RESEARCH_OR_DISPOSITION | INVENTORIED_COMPOSITE_UNIT_ATOMIC_SUPPORT_UNASSESSED | - **X-05 Copying spec examples verbatim as test fixtures** — rejected: examples embed comments, which data MUST NOT contain (F-04/F-33). |
| R022:report:L157 | COMPOSITE_RESEARCH_OR_DISPOSITION | INVENTORIED_COMPOSITE_UNIT_ATOMIC_SUPPORT_UNASSESSED | - **I-01** Add the discovery state machine (plate → well → fields; b2f2raw root with series/numbered fallback; plain multiscales group; labels enumeration), citing F-22/F-23/F-24/F-25/F-27/F-19. |
| R022:report:L158 | COMPOSITE_RESEARCH_OR_DISPOSITION | INVENTORIED_COMPOSITE_UNIT_ATOMIC_SUPPORT_UNASSESSED | - **I-02** Add the coordinate pipeline specification (dataset-then-group order, identity default, relative-scale 1.0 fallback, path-vector decision), citing F-13/F-14/F-15. |
| R022:report:L159 | UNCERTAINTY_OR_NEGATIVE_SCOPE | INVENTORIED_COMPOSITE_UNIT_ATOMIC_SUPPORT_UNASSESSED | - **I-03** Add omero-absent rendering defaults and source the window semantics externally (currently unresolved, U-3), citing F-18. |
| R022:report:L160 | COMPOSITE_RESEARCH_OR_DISPOSITION | INVENTORIED_COMPOSITE_UNIT_ATOMIC_SUPPORT_UNASSESSED | - **I-04** Add a unit-handling policy (missing/custom units presentation; the capture's enumerated unit lists L171–172 can drive validation UX), citing F-09. |
| R022:report:L161 | COMPOSITE_RESEARCH_OR_DISPOSITION | INVENTORIED_COMPOSITE_UNIT_ATOMIC_SUPPORT_UNASSESSED | - **I-05** Add `dimension_names`↔`axes` and `ome.version` consistency checks with actionable messages, citing F-10/F-03. |
| R022:report:L162 | COMPOSITE_RESEARCH_OR_DISPOSITION | INVENTORIED_COMPOSITE_UNIT_ATOMIC_SUPPORT_UNASSESSED | - **I-06** Add a version-pinned key-spelling table (e.g., `maximumfieldcount`, `rowIndex`, `columnIndex`) with a watch for future renames, citing F-28. |
| R022:report:L163 | COMPOSITE_RESEARCH_OR_DISPOSITION | INVENTORIED_COMPOSITE_UNIT_ATOMIC_SUPPORT_UNASSESSED | - **I-07** Add label-overlay validation (level-count parity, integer dtype whitelist, colors/alpha fallback), citing F-19/F-20/F-07/F-21. |
| R022:report:L164 | COMPOSITE_RESEARCH_OR_DISPOSITION | INVENTORIED_COMPOSITE_UNIT_ATOMIC_SUPPORT_UNASSESSED | - **I-08** Add malformed-input fixtures to acceptance: comments in JSON, inconsistent ome.version, axis-order violations, duplicate row/column names, path/index mismatch, wrong scale-vector length, citing F-04/F-03/F-11/F-22/F-13. |
| R022:report:L165 | COMPOSITE_RESEARCH_OR_DISPOSITION | INVENTORIED_COMPOSITE_UNIT_ATOMIC_SUPPORT_UNASSESSED | - **I-09** Add a scope statement on transitional/pre-0.5 data (support level, detection, messaging), citing F-24/F-29/C-1. |
| R022:report:L169 | COMPOSITE_RESEARCH_OR_DISPOSITION | INVENTORIED_COMPOSITE_UNIT_ATOMIC_SUPPORT_UNASSESSED | The thin plan is directionally consistent with the format but omits: multi-layout discovery (collections, HCS), the transform-composition pipeline, path-stored transform vectors, omero-absent fallbacks, unit-absent handling, key-spelling pinning, version-consistency and dimension_names checks, and any stance on transitional/legacy data. All of these are conditions or obligations the admitted evidence attaches to the plan's own promises (R01/R09). |
| R022:report:L173 | COMPOSITE_RESEARCH_OR_DISPOSITION | INVENTORIED_COMPOSITE_UNIT_ATOMIC_SUPPORT_UNASSESSED | No test, simulation or measurement has been run in this Goal; no execution evidence exists. The corpus contains no filesets to test against. |
| R022:report:L175 | PROPOSAL_OR_UNEXECUTED_TEST | INVENTORIED_COMPOSITE_UNIT_ATOMIC_SUPPORT_UNASSESSED | - **V-01 (UNEXECUTED)** Author a synthetic conformance matrix: valid filesets — 2D/3D/5D multiscales; with/without `omero`; with/without labels; time/channel/custom axis combinations; plate+well incl. sparse plate; bioformats2raw collection with and without `series`; path-stored scale vectors; missing units; custom axis types. Invalid filesets — one per violated MUST: inconsistent `ome.version`; comments in JSON; `dimension_names` missing/mismatched; axis-order violation; dataset path order/dim-count violations; scale-vector length mismatch; non-integer label pixels; metadata-bearing intermediate labels group; duplicate row names; well path↔row/column mismatch; acquisition key missing with multiple acquisitions. Expected results: discovery lists the correct image set; coordinates match hand-computed transforms; overlays align per level; failures name the offending image/data. |
| R022:report:L176 | PROPOSAL_OR_UNEXECUTED_TEST | INVENTORIED_COMPOSITE_UNIT_ATOMIC_SUPPORT_UNASSESSED | - **V-02 (UNEXECUTED)** Coordinate calibration check: compose dataset+multiscales transforms by hand for a known fixture and compare against the details-panel readout (validates F-13/F-14 implementation). |
| R022:report:L177 | PROPOSAL_OR_UNEXECUTED_TEST | INVENTORIED_COMPOSITE_UNIT_ATOMIC_SUPPORT_UNASSESSED | - **V-03 (UNEXECUTED)** Overlay alignment check: for each level i, verify label level i and image level i map to the same physical region (validates F-19/F-21 handling). |
| R022:report:L178 | PROPOSAL_OR_UNEXECUTED_TEST | INVENTORIED_COMPOSITE_UNIT_ATOMIC_SUPPORT_UNASSESSED | - **V-04 (UNEXECUTED)** Responsiveness check on a large synthetic pyramid: interaction remains within a latency budget while background reads run; changing views cancels pending chunk work (plan acceptance; no large dataset exists in this corpus). |
| R022:report:L179 | PROPOSAL_OR_UNEXECUTED_TEST | INVENTORIED_COMPOSITE_UNIT_ATOMIC_SUPPORT_UNASSESSED | - **V-05 (UNEXECUTED — blocked)** Interop spot-checks against filesets produced by real OME-Zarr 0.5 tools: **blocked** — no real tool output is admitted to this corpus and live acquisition is forbidden (F-31, U-1). Requires externally admitted samples; "interoperates with real tools" remains unverified. |

| R013:input:brief.md:L001 | SUPPLIED_PRODUCT_REQUIREMENT_OR_ASSIGNMENT_SCOPE | INPUT_BOUNDARY_NOT_CANDIDATE_ACQUISITION; IMPLEMENTATION_AND_TEST_EXECUTION_UNASSESSED | # Slide Scout product brief |
| R013:input:brief.md:L003 | SUPPLIED_PRODUCT_REQUIREMENT_OR_ASSIGNMENT_SCOPE | INPUT_BOUNDARY_NOT_CANDIDATE_ACQUISITION; IMPLEMENTATION_AND_TEST_EXECUTION_UNASSESSED | Slide Scout is a small read-only desktop browser for local OME-Zarr 0.5 bioimaging filesets. Scientists should be able to find images within a fileset, inspect multiresolution images by channel, time point and plane, overlay associated label images, and read correctly calibrated coordinates. It should remain responsive while opening large saved datasets and clearly explain data it cannot display. |
| R013:input:brief.md:L005 | SUPPLIED_PRODUCT_REQUIREMENT_OR_ASSIGNMENT_SCOPE | INPUT_BOUNDARY_NOT_CANDIDATE_ACQUISITION; IMPLEMENTATION_AND_TEST_EXECUTION_UNASSESSED | The current product boundary is local filesystem reading. Source files must remain unchanged. Image editing, export, remote services and clinical interpretation are outside this case. Research the format and actual reader implementations broadly enough to uncover compatibility, correctness, navigation and display requirements that a thin initial plan may miss. Distinguish established format obligations from optional capabilities and choices that need a product decision. |
| R013:input:TASK.md:L001 | SUPPLIED_PRODUCT_REQUIREMENT_OR_ASSIGNMENT_SCOPE | INPUT_BOUNDARY_NOT_CANDIDATE_ACQUISITION; IMPLEMENTATION_AND_TEST_EXECUTION_UNASSESSED | # Research assignment |
| R013:input:TASK.md:L003 | SUPPLIED_PRODUCT_REQUIREMENT_OR_ASSIGNMENT_SCOPE | INPUT_BOUNDARY_NOT_CANDIDATE_ACQUISITION; IMPLEMENTATION_AND_TEST_EXECUTION_UNASSESSED | Use the product brief, frozen thin plan and admitted primary-source capture to investigate the format obligations and implementation requirements that this viewer plan needs. Cover the whole brief within the assigned source scope, cite exact source lines, and distinguish established obligations from optional capabilities and product decisions. Preserve governing conditions, unsupported-input limitations, counterevidence and uncertainty. Proposed validation is UNEXECUTED unless actual execution evidence exists. Deliver a standalone current research report with complete findings, plan implications/disposition, citations and unresolved areas. Do not interpret task-card instructions as evidence about the format. |
| R013:input:TASK.md:L005 | SUPPLIED_PRODUCT_REQUIREMENT_OR_ASSIGNMENT_SCOPE | INPUT_BOUNDARY_NOT_CANDIDATE_ACQUISITION; IMPLEMENTATION_AND_TEST_EXECUTION_UNASSESSED | The source scope is only `sources/S003.txt`, a frozen OME-Zarr 0.5 normative capture. The plan is deliberately thin. Do not fetch live sources for this bounded assignment. |
| R013:input:TASK.md:L007 | SUPPLIED_PRODUCT_REQUIREMENT_OR_ASSIGNMENT_SCOPE | INPUT_BOUNDARY_NOT_CANDIDATE_ACQUISITION; IMPLEMENTATION_AND_TEST_EXECUTION_UNASSESSED | For coherent batching experiments the permitted brief-derived assignment groups are: |
| R013:input:TASK.md:L008 | SUPPLIED_PRODUCT_REQUIREMENT_OR_ASSIGNMENT_SCOPE | INPUT_BOUNDARY_NOT_CANDIDATE_ACQUISITION; IMPLEMENTATION_AND_TEST_EXECUTION_UNASSESSED | 1. Opening a fileset, listing/selecting images, and navigating multiresolution data. |
| R013:input:TASK.md:L009 | SUPPLIED_PRODUCT_REQUIREMENT_OR_ASSIGNMENT_SCOPE | INPUT_BOUNDARY_NOT_CANDIDATE_ACQUISITION; IMPLEMENTATION_AND_TEST_EXECUTION_UNASSESSED | 2. Channel/time/plane display, coordinate details, and associated label overlays. |
| R013:input:TASK.md:L011 | SUPPLIED_PRODUCT_REQUIREMENT_OR_ASSIGNMENT_SCOPE | INPUT_BOUNDARY_NOT_CANDIDATE_ACQUISITION; IMPLEMENTATION_AND_TEST_EXECUTION_UNASSESSED | Each group is evaluated against its own assigned part of the brief. The aggregate of groups has exactly the same full scope as a single combined assignment. Other methods cover both groups together. |
| R013:input:plan/Viewer.md:L001 | SUPPLIED_PRODUCT_REQUIREMENT_OR_ASSIGNMENT_SCOPE | INPUT_BOUNDARY_NOT_CANDIDATE_ACQUISITION; IMPLEMENTATION_AND_TEST_EXECUTION_UNASSESSED | # Slide Scout — initial viewer plan |
| R013:input:plan/Viewer.md:L003 | SUPPLIED_PRODUCT_REQUIREMENT_OR_ASSIGNMENT_SCOPE | INPUT_BOUNDARY_NOT_CANDIDATE_ACQUISITION; IMPLEMENTATION_AND_TEST_EXECUTION_UNASSESSED | This is a deliberately thin synthetic planning fixture for a research-workflow evaluation, not a Puppet Master feature or a specification-completeness claim. |
| R013:input:plan/Viewer.md:L005 | SUPPLIED_PRODUCT_REQUIREMENT_OR_ASSIGNMENT_SCOPE | INPUT_BOUNDARY_NOT_CANDIDATE_ACQUISITION; IMPLEMENTATION_AND_TEST_EXECUTION_UNASSESSED | The desktop application opens a local OME-Zarr 0.5 fileset and lists the images it finds. Selecting an image opens a canvas with pan and zoom, channel visibility controls, time-point and plane selection where relevant, and optional overlays for associated label images. A details panel shows dimensions, units and coordinates. The viewer chooses an available pyramid level appropriate for the current view. |
| R013:input:plan/Viewer.md:L007 | SUPPLIED_PRODUCT_REQUIREMENT_OR_ASSIGNMENT_SCOPE | INPUT_BOUNDARY_NOT_CANDIDATE_ACQUISITION; IMPLEMENTATION_AND_TEST_EXECUTION_UNASSESSED | Large image reads run in the background so navigation remains responsive. Changing the selected view cancels work that is no longer needed. Opening failures explain which image or data could not be displayed and allow the user to choose another item. |
| R013:input:plan/Viewer.md:L009 | SUPPLIED_PRODUCT_REQUIREMENT_OR_ASSIGNMENT_SCOPE | INPUT_BOUNDARY_NOT_CANDIDATE_ACQUISITION; IMPLEMENTATION_AND_TEST_EXECUTION_UNASSESSED | The initial implementation should interoperate with filesets from real OME-Zarr 0.5 tools. Source files remain unchanged; display settings are local to the viewing session. Image editing, export, remote storage and automated scientific or clinical interpretation are outside this plan. Additional capability choices require explicit review. |
| R013:input:plan/Viewer.md:L011 | SUPPLIED_PRODUCT_REQUIREMENT_OR_ASSIGNMENT_SCOPE | INPUT_BOUNDARY_NOT_CANDIDATE_ACQUISITION; IMPLEMENTATION_AND_TEST_EXECUTION_UNASSESSED | Acceptance uses representative filesets to check image discovery, navigation, channel and plane controls, calibrated coordinate display and aligned label overlays. A large dataset must not freeze interaction. Malformed or unavailable input must produce understandable feedback rather than a crash. |

Scientists should be able to find images within a fileset, inspect multiresolution images by channel, time point and plane, overlay associated label images, and read correctly calibrated coordinates. It should remain responsive while opening large saved datasets and clearly explain data it cannot display. |
| R014:input:brief.md:L005 | SUPPLIED_PRODUCT_REQUIREMENT_OR_ASSIGNMENT_SCOPE | INPUT_BOUNDARY_NOT_CANDIDATE_ACQUISITION; IMPLEMENTATION_AND_TEST_EXECUTION_UNASSESSED | The current product boundary is local filesystem reading. Source files must remain unchanged. Image editing, export, remote services and clinical interpretation are outside this case. Research the format and actual reader implementations broadly enough to uncover compatibility, correctness, navigation and display requirements that a thin initial plan may miss. Distinguish established format obligations from optional capabilities and choices that need a product decision. |

| R014:input:plan/Viewer.md:L007 | SUPPLIED_PRODUCT_REQUIREMENT_OR_ASSIGNMENT_SCOPE | INPUT_BOUNDARY_NOT_CANDIDATE_ACQUISITION; IMPLEMENTATION_AND_TEST_EXECUTION_UNASSESSED | Large image reads run in the background so navigation remains responsive. Additional capability choices require explicit review. |
| R014:input:plan/Viewer.md:L011 | SUPPLIED_PRODUCT_REQUIREMENT_OR_ASSIGNMENT_SCOPE | INPUT_BOUNDARY_NOT_CANDIDATE_ACQUISITION; IMPLEMENTATION_AND_TEST_EXECUTION_UNASSESSED | Acceptance uses representative filesets to check image discovery, navigation, channel and plane controls, calibrated coordinate display and aligned label overlays. A large dataset must not freeze interaction. Malformed or unavailable input must produce understandable feedback rather than a crash. |

## Freeze boundary

This review is frozen within the original unchanged 90-minute clock and 120-event conservative response allowance. Finalization occurred at the response reserve boundary while wall time remained. Atomic support coverage is incomplete, so no whole-case pass, method success, preservation percentage or same-quality efficiency winner is claimed. Review boundaries and user pauses are not candidate failures. Exact statement, source-context, transition, cost and evidence tables are pinned by final-freeze.json.
